### 12.13 Acceptance tests for edge-case, improvement, and polish requirements (AT, E/I/P)

One acceptance test per P0 and P1 requirement of spec parts E, I and P, in matrix order (Part 15.2-15.4). Columns and Method forms are as in 12.12. A Method cell may combine forms, main form first, separated by `; ` (for example `auto: IT-012; manual: XT-030`); every form in the cell is part of the acceptance test.

#### 12.13.1 Edge cases and failure modes (E)

Each row is the acceptance test of one P0 or P1 requirement of Part 15.2 (spec part E), run on the test build (`FingerLickinMusic.test.html`) with the fault hooks of Part 10.9 and the corpus of Part 8.9 unless the row names the shipped file or a document, and its Method names the automated tests of 12.2 to 12.7, the device and live tests of 12.8 and 12.9, or a document review.

#### 12.13.2 Essential product improvements (I)

One acceptance test per P0 and P1 requirement of spec part I (Part 15.3), in Part 15 order; "test build" means `FingerLickinMusic.test.html`, "delivered file" means `FingerLickinMusic.html`, and every auto row runs through `node tools/selftest.mjs` or `node tools/perf.mjs` unless the row names a manual or review step.

#### 12.13.3 Maximum-polish checklist (P)

One row per P0 and P1 requirement of Part 15.4, in matrix order; device classes (`ref-desktop`, `low-desktop`, `ref-ios`, `old-ios`, `ref-android`, `low-android`, `tablet`) are those of Part 9.2, and Method names the tests of 12.2 to 12.9 whose results decide the row, or `review` for plan, documentation and process obligations.
